- **hrms-activity-logging**: Any new deletion/restoration, ownership/role/assignment-change, or login/logout action in HRMS must also write a row to `user_activity_details` via `app.services.activity_log_service.log_activity` (or `log_login`/`log_logout`). This is IN ADDITION TO the automatic per-request row the activity-logging middleware already writes for every authenticated call — the manual call captures detail (which record, not just which URL) the middleware can't see. Use whenever adding a new soft-delete/archive action, a new "assign X to Y"/ ownership-transfer action, or touching auth login/logout. - **hrms-date-format**: Every user-facing date in HRMS displays as dd-MMM-yy (e.g. 15-Jul-23), and every date INPUT uses the shared hrms/frontend/src/components/ui/DateField.tsx component instead of a bare `&lt;input type="date"&gt;`. A native date input cannot be restyled to dd-MMM-yy with CSS — it always renders in the browser/OS locale format (MM/DD/YYYY, DD/MM/YYYY, etc.) — so DateField wraps it: a dd-MMM-yy text display up front, the real native input invisibly on top so the OS calendar picker still opens on click. Use whenever displaying a date/timestamp, or adding/reviewing any date-picker field, anywhere in `hrms/… ([gowthamraja-dev/oyehr-notes](https://github.com/gowthamraja-dev/oyehr-notes/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hrms-date-format))

Only after the schema is approved, write the plan (`.plans/&lt;feature&gt;-plan.md`) and split it into task files via `hrms-task-tracker`.
